### [Fragments: March 10](https://martinfowler.com/fragments/2026-03-10.html)

**来源**: Martin Fowler

**摘要**: <p><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/mar/04/california-tech-gofan-fine">Tech firm fined $1.1m by California for selling high-school students’ data</a></p>

<p>I agree with <a href="https://mstdn.social/@marick/116173536550791837">Brian Marick’s response</a></p>

<blockquote>
  <p>No such story should be published without a comparison of the fine to the company’s previous year revenue and profits, or valuation of last funding round. (I could only find a valuation of $11.0M in 2017.)</p>

  <p>We desperately need corporations’ attitudes to shift from “lawbreaking is a low-risk cost of doing business; we get a net profit anyway” to “this could be a death sentence.”</p>
</blockquote>

<p> ❄                ❄                ❄                ❄                ❄</p>

<p>Charity Majors gave the closing keynote at SRECon last year, encouraging people to engage with generative AI.</p>

<blockquote>
  <p>If I was giving the keynote at SRECon 2026, I would ditch the begrudging stance. I would start by acknowledging that AI is radically changing the way we build software. It’s here, it’s happening, and it is coming for us all.</p>
</blockquote>

<p>Her <a href="https://charitydotwtf.substack.com/p/my-hypothetical-srecon26-keynote">agenda this year</a> would be to tell everyone that they mustn’t wait for the wave to crash on them, but to swim out to meet it. In particular, I appreciated her call to resist our confirmation bias:</p>

<blockquote>
  <p>The best advice I can give anyone is: know your nature, and lean against it.</p>

  <ul>
    <li>If you are a reflexive naysayer or a pessimist, know that, and force yourself to find a way in to wonder, surprise and delight.</li>
    <li>If you are an optimist who gets very excited and tends to assume that everything will improve: know that, and force yourself to mind real cautionary tales.</li>
  </ul>
</blockquote>

<p> ❄                ❄                ❄                ❄                ❄</p>

<p>In a comment to Kief Morris’s recent article on <a href="https://martinfowler.com/articles/exploring-gen-ai/humans-and-agents.html">Humans and Agents in Software Loops</a>, in LinkedIn comments <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/feed/update/urn:li:activity:7434980139425349632?commentUrn=urn%3Ali%3Acomment%3A%28activity%3A7434980139425349632%2C7435048661711151105%29&amp;dashCommentUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_comment%3A%287435048661711151105%2Curn%3Ali%3Aactivity%3A7434980139425349632%29">Renaud Wilsius</a> may have coined another bit of terminology for the agent+programmer age</p>

<blockquote>
  <p>This completes the story of productivity, but it opens a new chapter on talent: The Apprentice Gap. If we move humans ‘on the loop’ too early in their careers, we risk a future where no one understands the ‘How’ deeply enough to build a robust harness. To manage the flywheel effectively, you still need the intuition that comes from having once been ‘in the loop.’ The next great challenge for CTOs isn’t just Harness Engineering, it’s ‘Experience Engineering’ for our junior developers in an agentic world.</p>
</blockquote>

<p> ❄                ❄                ❄                ❄                ❄</p>

<p>In hearing conversations about “the ralph loop”, I often hear it in the sense of just letting the agents loose to run on their own. So it’s interesting to read the originator of the ralph loop <a href="https://ghuntley.com/loop/">point out:</a></p>

<blockquote>
  <p>It’s important to watch the loop as that is where your personal development and learning will come from. When you see a failure domain – put on your engineering hat and resolve the problem so it never happens again.</p>

  <p>In practice this means doing the loop manually via prompting or via automation with a pause that involves having to prcss CTRL+C to progress onto the next task. This is still ralphing as ralph is about getting the most out how the underlying models work through context engineering and that pattern is GENERIC and can be used for ALL TASKS.</p>
</blockquote>

<p>At the <a href="https://martinfowler.com/bliki/FutureOfSoftwareDevelopment.html">Thoughtworks Future of Software Development Retreat</a> we were very concerned about cognitive debt. Watching the loop during ralphing is a way to learn about what the agent is building, so that it can be directed effectively in the future.</p>

<p> ❄                ❄                ❄                ❄                ❄</p>

<p>Anthropic recently published a page on how AI helps <a href="https://claude.com/blog/how-ai-helps-break-cost-barrier-cobol-modernization">break the cost barrier to COBOL modernization</a>. Using AI to help migrate COBOL systems isn’t an new idea to my colleagues, who <a href="https://martinfowler.com/articles/legacy-modernization-gen-ai.html">shared their experiences</a> using AI for this task over a year ago. While Anthropic’s article is correct about the value of AI, <a href="https://www.thoughtworks.com/insights/articles/claude-code-cobol-modernization-reality">there’s more to the process</a> than throwing some COBOL at an LLM.</p>

<blockquote>
  <p>The assumption that AI can simply translate COBOL into Java treats modernization as a syntactic exercise, as though a system is nothing more than its source code. That premise is flawed.</p>

  <p>A direct translation would, in the best case scenario, faithfully reproduce existing architectural constraints, accumulated technical debt and outdated design decisions. It wouldn’t address weaknesses; it would restate them in a different language.</p>

  <p>…</p>

  <p>In practice, modernization is rarely about preserving the past in a new syntax. It’s about aligning systems with current market demands, infrastructure paradigms, software supply chains and operating models. Even if AI were eventually capable of highly reliable code translation, blind conversion would risk recreating the same system with the same limitations, in another language, without a deliberate strategy for replacing or retiring its legacy ecosystem.</p>
</blockquote>

<p> ❄                ❄                ❄                ❄                ❄</p>

<p><a href="https://mas.to/@inconvergent/116193880010747322">Anders Hoff</a> (inconvergent)</p>

<blockquote>
  <p>an LLM is a compiler in the same way that a slot machine is an ATM</p>
</blockquote>

<p> ❄                ❄                ❄                ❄                ❄</p>

<p>One of the more interesting aspects of the network of people around Jeffrey Epstein is how many people from academia were connected. It’s understandable why, he had a lot of money to offer, and most academics are always looking for funding for their work. Most of the attention on Epstein’s network focused on those that got involved with him, but I’m interested in those who kept their distance and why - so I enjoyed <a href="https://www.science.org/content/article/why-three-scientists-said-no-epstein">Jeffrey Mervis’s article in Science</a></p>

<blockquote>
  <p>Many of the scientists Epstein courted were already well-established and well-funded. So why didn’t they all just say no? Science talked with three who did just that. Here’s how Epstein approached them, and why they refused to have anything to do with him.</p>
</blockquote>

<p>I believe that keeping away from bad people makes life much more pleasant, if nothing else it reduces a lot of stress. So it’s good to understand how people make decisions on who to avoid.</p>

---

## LLM Infrastructure

### [v0.19.1rc0: [Misc] Clean up Gemma4 implementation (#38872)](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.19.1rc0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <p>Signed-off-by: Isotr0py <a href="mailto:mozf@mail2.sysu.edu.cn">mozf@mail2.sysu.edu.cn</a></p>

---

### [v0.19.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.19.0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>vLLM v0.19.0</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 448 commits from 197 contributors (54 new)!</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Gemma 4 support</strong>: Full Google Gemma 4 architecture support including MoE, multimodal, reasoning, and tool-use capabilities (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38826">#38826</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38847">#38847</a>). Requires <code>transformers&gt;=5.5.0</code>. We recommend using pre-built docker image <code>vllm/vllm-openai:gemma4</code> for out of box usage.</li>
<li><strong>Zero-bubble async scheduling + speculative decoding</strong>: Async scheduling now supports speculative decoding with zero-bubble overlap, significantly improving throughput (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32951">#32951</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2 maturation</strong>: MRV2 gains piecewise CUDA graphs for pipeline parallelism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35162">#35162</a>), spec decode rejection sampler with greedy/logprobs support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37238">#37238</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37237">#37237</a>), multi-modal embeddings for spec decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36097">#36097</a>), streaming inputs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37028">#37028</a>), and EPLB support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37488">#37488</a>).</li>
<li><strong>ViT Full CUDA Graphs</strong>: Vision encoders (ViT) now support full CUDA graph capture for reduced overhead (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35963">#35963</a>).</li>
<li><strong>General CPU KV cache offloading</strong>: A simple yet general CPU KV cache offloading mechanism for V1, with pluggable cache policy and block-level preemption handling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37160">#37160</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37874">#37874</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34805">#34805</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36642">#36642</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37853">#37853</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DBO (Dual-Batch Overlap) generalization</strong>: The microbatch optimization (DBO) now works with general models, not just specific architectures (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37926">#37926</a>).</li>
<li><strong>NVIDIA B300/GB300 (SM 10.3) support</strong>: Allreduce fusion enabled by default with tuned all-reduce communicator (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37755">#37755</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37756">#37756</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Transformers v5 compatibility</strong>: Broad compatibility fixes across many models for HuggingFace Transformers v5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37681">#37681</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38127">#38127</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38090">#38090</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38247">#38247</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38410">#38410</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Model Support</h3>
<ul>
<li>New architectures: Gemma 4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38826">#38826</a>), Cohere ASR (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35809">#35809</a>), Cohere Transcribe (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38120">#38120</a>), ColQwen3.5 4.5B (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36887">#36887</a>), LFM2-ColBERT-350M (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37528">#37528</a>), Granite 4.0 1B Speech (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38019">#38019</a>), Qwen3-ForcedAligner (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35367">#35367</a>).</li>
<li>Speculative decoding: Eagle3 for Pixtral (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37182">#37182</a>), EagleMistralLarge3 fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37232">#37232</a>).</li>
<li>LoRA expansion: H2OVL tower/connector LoRA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31696">#31696</a>), <code>--lora-target-modules</code> to restrict LoRA to specific modules (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34984">#34984</a>), <code>language_model_only</code> respected (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37375">#37375</a>), Mistral3 fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36928">#36928</a>), Qwen3.5 fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36976">#36976</a>), out-of-tree ops replacement (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37181">#37181</a>).</li>
<li>Model fixes: NemotronH MTP + Chunked Prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35447">#35447</a>), Qwen3-VL video timestamps (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37439">#37439</a>), Qwen3.5 GDN quantized models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37448">#37448</a>), Qwen3Next A_log FP32 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37810">#37810</a>), JAIS ALiBi (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37820">#37820</a>), RoBERTa CUDA graph position IDs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37873">#37873</a>), AudioFlamingo3/MusicFlamingo (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37643">#37643</a>), Music Flamingo loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35535">#35535</a>), bge-m3 task selection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37632">#37632</a>), Nemotron Parse loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37407">#37407</a>), GLM OCR patch merger (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37962">#37962</a>), PaddleOCR checkpoint compat (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38232">#38232</a>), DeepSeek v3.2 params (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33703">#33703</a>), MiniMax NVFP4 weight loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37214">#37214</a>), gated model HF token (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37920">#37920</a>), Parakeet OOM on long audio (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36671">#36671</a>).</li>
<li>Features: Temporal compression for Nemotron-3-VL videos (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36808">#36808</a>), NemotronH Puzzle + MTP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37803">#37803</a>), torch.compile for InternVL vision encoder (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38049">#38049</a>), multiple embedding types in single call (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35829">#35829</a>).</li>
<li>Performance: GLM-4.xv ViT optimization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37779">#37779</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Engine Core</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Zero-bubble async scheduling + speculative decoding</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32951">#32951</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2</strong>: PP CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35162">#35162</a>), spec decode rejection sampler greedy (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37238">#37238</a>) + logprobs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37237">#37237</a>), multimodal embeddings for spec decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36097">#36097</a>), streaming inputs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37028">#37028</a>), configurable acceptance rate (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38045">#38045</a>), FP32 draft logits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37526">#37526</a>), FP64 Gumbel noise (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37798">#37798</a>), warmup with spec decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37812">#37812</a>).</li>
<li><strong>ViT Full CUDA Graph</strong> capture (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35963">#35963</a>).</li>
<li><strong>General CPU KV cache offloading</strong> with pluggable CachePolicy (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37160">#37160</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37874">#37874</a>), block-level preemption (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34805">#34805</a>), multiple KV groups (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36642">#36642</a>), hybrid model support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37853">#37853</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DBO for general models</strong>: Microbatch optimization generalized beyond specific architectures (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37926">#37926</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Compilation</strong>: Mega AOT artifact for torch 2.12+ (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37198">#37198</a>), lazy graph module to defer recompile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37609">#37609</a>), remove model tag requirement for compile cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37345">#37345</a>), Triton autotuning disk cache enabled by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37188">#37188</a>), inductor runtime asserts disabled by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37485">#37485</a>).</li>
<li><strong>FlexAttention</strong>: Custom mask modification support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37692">#37692</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Attention</strong>: Distinguish short extends vs decodes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37303">#37303</a>), allow qk_nope_head_dim=192 in FlashInfer MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37475">#37475</a>), skip sliding window attention layers with FP8 KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33695">#33695</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Scheduling</strong>: Schedule requests based on full input sequence length (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37307">#37307</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Spec decode</strong>: Per-draft-model MoE backend via <code>--speculative-config</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37880">#37880</a>), Eagle3 drafter quant_config propagation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37280">#37280</a>), Eagle3 norm_before_fc propagation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38111">#38111</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Extensibility</strong>: PluggableLayer for CustomQwen2Decoder (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37293">#37293</a>), tensor IPC transfer for multimodal data (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32104">#32104</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Performance</strong>: Optimize top-k in Triton sampler (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37225">#37225</a>), optimize token_embed for pooling models with 1% improvement (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37347">#37347</a>), fix slow hasattr in CUDAGraphWrapper (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37425">#37425</a>), NFS prefetch auto-enabled with RAM guard (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37673">#37673</a>), pybase64 replacement (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37290">#37290</a>), optimize swap_states for hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34733">#34733</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Bugfixes</strong>: Fix gibberish from FP8 MLA KV scale inconsistency (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37054">#37054</a>), Mamba state corruption (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37728">#37728</a>), deadlock with pause/resume (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37024">#37024</a>), FlashInfer MNNVL socket collisions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36674">#36674</a>), multimodal prefix cache key collisions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36708">#36708</a>), DP coordinator ZMQ TOCTOU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37452">#37452</a>), CUDA graph memory double-counting (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37426">#37426</a>), pooling non-determinism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37775">#37775</a>), AllReduce Fusion shutdown crash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36955">#36955</a>), FlashInfer allreduce workspace (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37461">#37461</a>), async spec decoding with hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38556">#38556</a>), MLA sparse indexer prefill chunking (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36178">#36178</a>), KV offloading + MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37536">#37536</a>), async scheduling extra CUDA context (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37449">#37449</a>), DP MTP dummy run (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35243">#35243</a>), offloading+prefetch for GLM-4.7-FP8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37178">#37178</a>), max memory for multiple KV-cache groups (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36030">#36030</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Hardware &amp; Performance</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>NVIDIA</strong>:
<ul>
<li>B300/GB300 (SM 10.3): Allreduce fusion enabled by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37755">#37755</a>), tuned all-reduce communicator (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37756">#37756</a>).</li>
<li>Blackwell: Optimized SM120 CUTLASS blockwise FP8 GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37970">#37970</a>), fix NVFP4 NaN on desktop Blackwell (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37725">#37725</a>), fix DeepGEMM E8M0 accuracy for Qwen3.5 FP8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38083">#38083</a>), restore FP8 FlashMLA CUDA graph persistent buffers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35175">#35175</a>), DGX Spark fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38126">#38126</a>).</li>
<li>FlashInfer sparse MLA as default for FP8 KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37252">#37252</a>).</li>
<li>Tuned prefill configs for FP8 FA3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36265">#36265</a>), tuned Triton MoE config for Qwen3.5 on H200 with 9.9% E2E improvement (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37340">#37340</a>), H800 MoE configs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31201">#31201</a>).</li>
<li>GPT-OSS: Router GEMM kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37205">#37205</a>), eliminate padding with FlashInfer MXFP4/MXFP8 MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30647">#30647</a>), reduce redundant SparseMatrix creation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37683">#37683</a>).</li>
<li>NVFP4 CUTLASS MoE non-gated support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37320">#37320</a>), fuse pack topk in TRTLLM MoE via torch.compile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37695">#37695</a>).</li>
<li>Non-contiguous KV cache in TRTLLM FP8 dequant kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36867">#36867</a>), Qwen3 dual stream input projection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36795">#36795</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>AMD ROCm</strong>:
<ul>
<li>ROCm 7.2.1, torch 2.10, triton 3.6 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38252">#38252</a>).</li>
<li>DeepEP as all2all backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34692">#34692</a>).</li>
<li>Persistent MLA kernel from AITER (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36574">#36574</a>), FP8xFP8 attention in AITER (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36927">#36927</a>).</li>
<li>AWQ Marlin support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36505">#36505</a>), wvSplitK skinny GEMM for RDNA4/gfx1x (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34709">#34709</a>).</li>
<li>Nightly Docker image and wheel releases (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37283">#37283</a>).</li>
<li>Bugfixes: Sleep mode memory leak (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37533">#37533</a>), hybrid model stride (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37228">#37228</a>), qwen3_next crash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36795">#36795</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Intel XPU</strong>: MLA model support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37143">#37143</a>), CompressedTensor W4A8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37207">#37207</a>), auto-detect XPU build platform (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37634">#37634</a>).</li>
<li><strong>TPU</strong>: Async scheduling interface (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36924">#36924</a>), Qwen3.5 FP8 weight loading fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37348">#37348</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CPU</strong>: Enable tcmalloc by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37607">#37607</a>), graceful degradation without tcmalloc/libiomp (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37561">#37561</a>), 48.9% throughput improvement for pooling models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38139">#38139</a>), OpenMP thread fix for torch.compile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37538">#37538</a>), structured output crash fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37706">#37706</a>), KV cache block zeroing crash fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37550">#37550</a>), slot mapping kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37987">#37987</a>), W4A16 compressed tensors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38219">#38219</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Performance fixes</strong>: FP8 DeepGEMM batch invariance (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37718">#37718</a>), Triton autotuning for Qwen3.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37338">#37338</a>), TRTLLM NVFP4 routing precision (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36725">#36725</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Large Scale Serving</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Disaggregated serving</strong>: PD kv_transfer_params for Anthropic Messages (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37535">#37535</a>) and Responses API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37424">#37424</a>), Mooncake heterogeneous TP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36869">#36869</a>), Mamba N-1 prefill for P/D (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37310">#37310</a>).</li>
<li><strong>EPLB</strong>: MRV2 support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37488">#37488</a>), improved responsiveness (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36271">#36271</a>), EP weight filter fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37322">#37322</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Elastic EP</strong>: Fix repeated scale up/down cycles (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37131">#37131</a>), fix stateless group port races (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36330">#36330</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DBO</strong>: Generalized to work with all models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37926">#37926</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Multi-node</strong>: Fix allreduce fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38136">#38136</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV connector</strong>: Plugin-overridable metadata build (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37336">#37336</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Constraints</strong>: Cap API servers to 1 with Elastic EP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37466">#37466</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Quantization</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Online MXFP8</strong> quantization for MoE and dense models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35448">#35448</a>).</li>
<li><strong>FP8</strong>: WoQ kernel abstraction (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32929">#32929</a>), Marlin FP8 for compressed tensors fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38092">#38092</a>).</li>
<li><strong>NVFP4</strong>: Rescale weight scales to fix BF16 dequant underflow (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34577">#34577</a>), fix Marlin NaN/Inf with float16 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33972">#33972</a>).</li>
<li><strong>QeRL</strong>: Online quantization composed with quantized reloading for RLHF (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38032">#38032</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CPU</strong>: W4A16 compressed tensors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38219">#38219</a>).</li>
<li><strong>XPU</strong>: CompressedTensor W4A8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37207">#37207</a>).</li>
<li><strong>ROCm</strong>: AWQ Marlin support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36505">#36505</a>).</li>
<li><strong>MXFP8 + DeepGEMM</strong>: Fix crash when both are active (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37358">#37358</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Removals</strong>: Per-tensor-per-channel FP8 removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32700">#32700</a>), Sparse24 integration and kernels removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36799">#36799</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>API &amp; Frontend</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>New endpoints</strong>: <code>/v1/chat/completions/batch</code> for batched chat completions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38011">#38011</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Features</strong>: Limit thinking tokens (hard limit) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20859">#20859</a>), multiple embedding types in single call (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35829">#35829</a>), numpy array embeddings for multimodal (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38119">#38119</a>), <code>--lora-target-modules</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34984">#34984</a>), <code>-sc</code> shorthand for <code>--speculative-config</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38380">#38380</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Tool parsing</strong>: GigaChat 3.1 parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36664">#36664</a>), Kimi-K2.5 reasoning/tool parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37438">#37438</a>), Gemma 4 tool parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38847">#38847</a>), tools passed to parser constructor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38029">#38029</a>), fix Mistral parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37209">#37209</a>), fix DeepSeek v3.2 streaming (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36056">#36056</a>), fix GLM-4.7 parsing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37386">#37386</a>), fix Hermes streaming (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38168">#38168</a>), fix OpenAI tool parser IndexError (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37958">#37958</a>), fix Anthropic streaming (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37510">#37510</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Responses API</strong>: Fix crash with tool_choice=required exceeding max_output_tokens (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37258">#37258</a>), fix TTFT recording (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37498">#37498</a>), fix Anthropic serving template kwargs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37899">#37899</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Performance</strong>: Offload blocking tokenizer ops to thread pool (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34789">#34789</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Deprecations</strong>: <code>--calculate-kv-scales</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37201">#37201</a>), <code>score</code> task (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37537">#37537</a>), pooling multi-task support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37956">#37956</a>), <code>reasoning_content</code> message field removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37480">#37480</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Bugfixes</strong>: Embed/classify task routing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37573">#37573</a>), Cohere embed task instruction (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38362">#38362</a>), renderer workers restricted to 1 with MM cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38418">#38418</a>).</li>
<li><strong>UX</strong>: Log once per node by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37568">#37568</a>), torch profiler with stack enabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37571">#37571</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Security</h3>
<ul>
<li>Add <code>VLLM_MAX_N_SEQUENCES</code> environment variable to enforce sequence limits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37952">#37952</a>).</li>
<li>Enforce frame limit in VideoMediaIO to prevent resource exhaustion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38636">#38636</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Dependencies</h3>
<ul>
<li>Transformers v5 compatibility across many models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37681">#37681</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38127">#38127</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38247">#38247</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38410">#38410</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38090">#38090</a>).</li>
<li>ROCm 7.2.1, torch 2.10, triton 3.6 for ROCm builds (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38252">#38252</a>).</li>
<li>compressed-tensors bumped to 0.14.0.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36988">#36988</a>).</li>
<li>Python OpenAI package bumped (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32316">#32316</a>).</li>
<li>flashinfer-cubin added as default CUDA dependency (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37233">#37233</a>).</li>
<li>librosa removed from audio dependencies (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37058">#37058</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>V0 Deprecation</h3>
<ul>
<li>Deprecate virtual engine (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37195">#37195</a>).</li>
<li>Deprecate <code>--disable-frontend-multiprocessing</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37612">#37612</a>).</li>
<li>Refactor KV cache from list to element (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37487">#37487</a>).</li>
</ul>

### [v0.18.1](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.18.1)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <p>This is a patch release on top of v0.18.0 to address a few issues:</p>
<ul>
<li>Change default SM100 MLA prefill backend back to TRT-LLM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38562">#38562</a>)</li>
<li>Fix mock.patch resolution failure for standalone_compile.FakeTensorMode on Python &lt;= 3.10 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37158">#37158</a>)</li>
<li>Disable monolithic TRTLLM MoE for Renormalize routing <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37605">#37605</a></li>
<li>Pre-download missing FlashInfer headers in Docker build <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38391">#38391</a></li>
<li>Fix DeepGemm E8M0 accuracy degradation for Qwen3.5 FP8 on Blackwell (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38083">#38083</a>)</li>
</ul>

### [v0.18.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.18.0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>vLLM v0.18.0</h1>
<h2>Known issues</h2>
<ul>
<li>Degraded accuracy when serving Qwen3.5 with FP8 KV cache on B200 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/37618">#37618</a>)</li>
<li>If you previously ran into <code>CUBLAS_STATUS_INVALID_VALUE</code> and had to use a workaround in <code>v0.17.0</code>, you can reinstall <code>torch 2.10.0</code>. PyTorch published an updated wheel that addresses this bug.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 445 commits from 213 contributors (61 new)!</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>gRPC Serving Support</strong>: vLLM now supports gRPC serving via the new <code>--grpc</code> flag (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36169">#36169</a>), enabling high-performance RPC-based serving alongside the existing HTTP/REST interface.</li>
<li><strong>GPU-less Render Serving</strong>: New <code>vllm launch render</code> command (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36166">#36166</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34551">#34551</a>) enables GPU-less preprocessing and rendering, allowing separation of multimodal preprocessing from GPU inference.</li>
<li><strong>NGram GPU Speculative Decoding</strong>: NGram speculative decoding now runs on GPU and is compatible with the async scheduler (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29184">#29184</a>), significantly reducing spec decode overhead.</li>
<li><strong>KV Cache Offloading Improvements</strong>: Smart CPU offloading that stores only frequently-reused blocks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35342">#35342</a>), plus FlexKV as a new offloading backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34328">#34328</a>) and support for multiple KV groups in offloading spec (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36610">#36610</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Elastic Expert Parallelism Milestone 2</strong>: NIXL-EP integration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35627">#35627</a>) enables dynamic GPU scaling for MoE experts, with new <code>--enable-ep-weight-filter</code> CLI option (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37351">#37351</a>) for faster EP model loading.</li>
<li><strong>FlashInfer 0.6.6</strong>: Updated FlashInfer dependency (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36768">#36768</a>) with numerous performance and correctness improvements.</li>
<li><strong>Responses API Streaming Tool Calls</strong>: The OpenAI Responses API now supports tool/function calling with streaming (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29947">#29947</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Online Beam Search for ASR</strong>: Beam search support for encoder/decoder models both offline (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36153">#36153</a>) and online transcriptions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36160">#36160</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Ray No Longer a Default Dependency</strong>: Ray has been removed as a default dependency (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36170">#36170</a>) — install it explicitly if needed.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Model Support</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>New architectures</strong>: Sarvam MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33942">#33942</a>), OLMo Hybrid (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32550">#32550</a>), HyperCLOVAX-SEED-Think-32B VLM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31471">#31471</a>), HyperCLOVAX-SEED-Think-14B (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37107">#37107</a>), Kimi-Audio-7B-Instruct (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36127">#36127</a>), ColPali late-interaction retrieval (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36818">#36818</a>), ERNIE pooling models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36385">#36385</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Speculative decoding</strong>: Eagle3 for Qwen3.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36658">#36658</a>), Eagle3 for Kimi K2.5 MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36361">#36361</a>), Eagle for Mistral Large 3 with dense layers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36163">#36163</a>).</li>
<li><strong>LoRA</strong>: Whisper LoRA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29856">#29856</a>), FP8 LoRA dense kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35242">#35242</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Multimodal</strong>: Online use_audio_in_video (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36319">#36319</a>), audio extraction from MP4 for Nemotron Nano VL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35539">#35539</a>), audio transcription for MP4/M4A/WebM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35109">#35109</a>), expose media_io_kwargs at runtime (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34778">#34778</a>), fast media preprocessing for Nano Nemotron VL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35657">#35657</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Compatibility</strong>: Gemma/Gemma2 inputs_embeds (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36787">#36787</a>), SigLIP/CLIP Transformers v5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37200">#37200</a>), fused expert weights in Transformers backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36997">#36997</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Performance</strong>: Qwen3 Next fused GDN kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35777">#35777</a>), LFM2 tuned H100 MoE configs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36699">#36699</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Fixes</strong>: DeepSeek-V3.2 tokenizer space stripping (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37004">#37004</a>), Qwen3.5 tool calling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36774">#36774</a>), Qwen3-VL timestamp mismatch (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36136">#36136</a>), Qwen3-Next TP&gt;1 weight sharding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36242">#36242</a>), Qwen3-ASR torch.compile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35869">#35869</a>), MiniCPM-V audio inference (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36751">#36751</a>), MiniCPM-O 4.5 ViT attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34127">#34127</a>), routed experts for hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35744">#35744</a>), Qwen2.5-Omni/Qwen3-Omni multi-video audio_in_video (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37147">#37147</a>), DeepSeek-OCR empty images crash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36670">#36670</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Engine Core</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2</strong>: Probabilistic rejection sampling for spec decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35461">#35461</a>), pooling models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36019">#36019</a>), extensible CUDA graph dispatch (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35959">#35959</a>), WhisperModelState (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35790">#35790</a>), XD-RoPE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36817">#36817</a>), model_state CUDA graph capture (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36544">#36544</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV cache offloading</strong>: Reuse-frequency-gated CPU stores (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35342">#35342</a>), FlexKV offloading backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34328">#34328</a>), multiple KV groups (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36610">#36610</a>), async scheduling fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33881">#33881</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Speculative decoding</strong>: NGram GPU implementation with async scheduler (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29184">#29184</a>), fused EAGLE step slot mapping (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33503">#33503</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Performance</strong>: Remove busy loop from idle buffer readers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28053">#28053</a>), 2.7% E2E throughput for pooling via worker-side maxsim (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36159">#36159</a>), 3.2% via batched maxsim (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36710">#36710</a>), CUDA graph memory accounting during profiling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30515">#30515</a>), checkpoint prefetch to OS page cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36012">#36012</a>), InstantTensor weight loader (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36139">#36139</a>), sporadic stall fix via pin_memory removal (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37006">#37006</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Stability</strong>: VLM concurrent throughput degradation fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36557">#36557</a>), DP deadlock fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35194">#35194</a>), DeepSeek V3.2 OOM during CG profiling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36691">#36691</a>), Ray DP startup crash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36665">#36665</a>), NCCL rank calculation fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36940">#36940</a>), zero-init MLA output buffers for NaN prevention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37442">#37442</a>), CUDA OOM fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35594">#35594</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Defaults</strong>: Cascade attention disabled by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36318">#36318</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Extensibility</strong>: OOT linear method registration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35981">#35981</a>), custom collective ops registration for non-CUDA platforms (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34760">#34760</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Kernel</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>FA4 for MLA prefill</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34732">#34732</a>).</li>
<li><strong>FlashInfer Sparse MLA</strong>: FP8 KV cache support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35891">#35891</a>), CUDA graphs on ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35719">#35719</a>), MTP lens &gt; 1 on ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36681">#36681</a>).</li>
<li><strong>TRTLLM FP8 MoE modular kernel</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36307">#36307</a>).</li>
<li><strong>FP8 KV cache for Triton MLA decode</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34597">#34597</a>).</li>
<li><strong>FlashInfer MoE A2A kernel</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36022">#36022</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Remove chunking from FusedMoE</strong> for full batch processing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34086">#34086</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CustomOp FusedRMSNormGated</strong> for torch.compile compatibility (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35877">#35877</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Mamba2 SSD prefill Triton kernel</strong> optimization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35397">#35397</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DeepSeek-V3.2</strong>: Vectorized MLA query concat kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34917">#34917</a>), optimized FP8 KV cache gather for context parallel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35290">#35290</a>).</li>
<li><strong>320-dimension MLA head size</strong> support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36161">#36161</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Packed recurrent fast path</strong> for decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36596">#36596</a>).</li>
<li><strong>EP scatter race condition</strong> fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34991">#34991</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Hardware &amp; Performance</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>NVIDIA</strong>: FA4 for MLA prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34732">#34732</a>), DeepSeek-V3.2 MLA kernel optimizations (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34917">#34917</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35290">#35290</a>).</li>
<li><strong>AMD ROCm</strong>: Sparse MLA CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35719">#35719</a>), MTP lens &gt; 1 in Sparse MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36681">#36681</a>), MLA with nhead&lt;16 + FP8 KV for TP=8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35850">#35850</a>), RoPE+KV cache fusion for AITER FA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35786">#35786</a>), AITER MLA CPU sync avoidance (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35765">#35765</a>), Quark W4A8 MXFP4/FP8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35316">#35316</a>), gfx1152/gfx1153 Krackan support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36499">#36499</a>), fused_topk_bias AITER optimization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36253">#36253</a>), skinny GEMM improvements (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34304">#34304</a>), DeepEP in ROCm Dockerfile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36086">#36086</a>), startup OOM fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36720">#36720</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Intel XPU</strong>: Model Runner V2 enabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36078">#36078</a>), MLA Sparse backend for DeepSeek V3.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33230">#33230</a>), LoRA via torch.compile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36962">#36962</a>), block FP8 MoE fallback (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36458">#36458</a>), deepseek_scaling_rope fused kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36612">#36612</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CPU</strong>: aarch64 int8 matmul via OneDNN upgrade (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36147">#36147</a>), AMD Zen CPU backend via zentorch (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35970">#35970</a>).</li>
<li><strong>RISC-V</strong>: CPU backend support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36578">#36578</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Performance</strong>: 5% E2E improvement for PD disaggregation scheduling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35781">#35781</a>), packed recurrent decode fast path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36596">#36596</a>), pooling model maxsim 2.7%+3.2% throughput (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36159">#36159</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36710">#36710</a>).</li>
<li><strong>torch.compile</strong>: FakeTensors instead of real GPU tensors for single-size compilation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36093">#36093</a>), non-contiguous fused RMSNorm + group quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36551">#36551</a>), stop lazy compiling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35472">#35472</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Large Scale Serving</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Elastic EP Milestone 2</strong>: NIXL-EP integration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35627">#35627</a>), <code>--enable-ep-weight-filter</code> for faster EP loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37351">#37351</a>).</li>
<li><strong>PD Disaggregation</strong>: ~5% scheduler overhead reduction (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35781">#35781</a>), KV transfer fix with spec decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35158">#35158</a>), P/D for hybrid SSM-FA models via NIXL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36687">#36687</a>), PP for multimodal models on Transformers backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37057">#37057</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV Connectors</strong>: HMA + NIXL connector (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35758">#35758</a>), FlexKV offloading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34328">#34328</a>), worker→scheduler metadata (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31964">#31964</a>), All-to-All DCP backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34883">#34883</a>).</li>
<li><strong>LMCache</strong>: Fault tolerance mechanism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36586">#36586</a>), memory leak fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35931">#35931</a>), race condition fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35831">#35831</a>), TP size for MLA multi-reader locking (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36129">#36129</a>).</li>
<li><strong>EP loading</strong>: Skip non-local expert weights (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37136">#37136</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Quantization</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>ModelOpt MXFP8 MoE</strong> support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35986">#35986</a>).</li>
<li><strong>MXFP4 MoE routing simulation</strong> override for accuracy (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33595">#33595</a>).</li>
<li><strong>FP8 LoRA dense kernel</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35242">#35242</a>).</li>
<li><strong>ROCm</strong>: Quark W4A8 MXFP4/FP8 for LinearLayer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35316">#35316</a>), compressed-tensors fix for DeepSeek-R1 on MI300x (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36247">#36247</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Fixes</strong>: MLA crash with AWQ/GPTQ quantized models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34695">#34695</a>), score layer quantization for reranker models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35849">#35849</a>), GLM-4.1V non-default quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36321">#36321</a>), FP8 k_scale/v_scale loading for Qwen3-MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35656">#35656</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>API &amp; Frontend</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>gRPC</strong>: New <code>--grpc</code> flag for gRPC serving (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36169">#36169</a>).</li>
<li><strong>GPU-less serving</strong>: <code>vllm launch render</code> for preprocessing-only serving (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36166">#36166</a>), <code>vllm launch</code> for GPU-less preprocessing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34551">#34551</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Responses API</strong>: Streaming tool/function calling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29947">#29947</a>), reasoning item fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34499">#34499</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36516">#36516</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Anthropic API</strong>: Accept redacted thinking blocks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36992">#36992</a>).</li>
<li><strong>ASR</strong>: Online beam search transcriptions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36160">#36160</a>), offline beam search (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36153">#36153</a>), audio transcription for MP4/M4A/WebM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35109">#35109</a>), realtime endpoint metrics (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35500">#35500</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Tool calling</strong>: Granite4 tool parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36827">#36827</a>), Qwen3Coder anyOf double encoding fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36032">#36032</a>).</li>
<li><strong>New options</strong>: <code>--distributed-timeout-seconds</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36047">#36047</a>), <code>--attention-backend auto</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35738">#35738</a>), <code>reasoning_effort=none</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36238">#36238</a>), PyTorch profiler schedule (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35240">#35240</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Cohere Embed v2 API</strong> support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37074">#37074</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Azure Blob Storage</strong> support for RunAI Model Streamer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34614">#34614</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Graceful shutdown</strong> timeout for in-flight requests (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36666">#36666</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Fixes</strong>: tool_choice=required exceeding max_tokens crash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36841">#36841</a>), negative max_tokens with long prompts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36789">#36789</a>), concurrent classify/token_classify race (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36614">#36614</a>), Anthropic billing header prefix cache miss (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36829">#36829</a>), render endpoint crash for multimodal requests (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35684">#35684</a>), xgrammar dtype mismatch on macOS CPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32384">#32384</a>), minimax_m2 tool parser with stream interval &gt; 1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35895">#35895</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Security</h3>
<ul>
<li>Respect user <code>trust_remote_code</code> setting in NemotronVL and KimiK25 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36192">#36192</a>).</li>
<li>Upgrade xgrammar for security fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36168">#36168</a>).</li>
<li>Guard RLHF weight sync deserialization behind insecure serialization flag (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35928">#35928</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Dependencies</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>FlashInfer 0.6.6</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36768">#36768</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Ray removed from default dependencies</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36170">#36170</a>).</li>
<li><code>kaldi_native_fbank</code> made optional (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35996">#35996</a>).</li>
<li>OpenAI dependency bounded to 2.24.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36471">#36471</a>).</li>
<li>Deprecated items from v0.18 removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36470">#36470</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36006">#36006</a>).</li>
<li>Mistral common v10 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36971">#36971</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Breaking Changes</h3>
<ol>
<li><strong>Ray no longer a default dependency</strong> — install explicitly if needed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36170">#36170</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Deprecated items removed</strong> — items deprecated in v0.18 have been removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36470">#36470</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36006">#36006</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Cascade attention disabled by default</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36318">#36318</a>).</li>
<li><strong>swap_space parameter removed</strong> (V0 deprecation, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36216">#36216</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Monolithic TRTLLM MoE disabled for renormalize routing</strong> — late fix cherry-picked (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/37591">#37591</a>).</li>
</ol>
